| `CL-B0243-071` | A collective proposal takes effect through support, irritation and practical mediation. `THEMATIC_INTERPRETATION`. | Hina explicitly endorses handholding to the photographer. Ume predicts Emoco will mock it; Emoco actually calls the idea lovely while adding insults. Ume threatens to exclude her, Emoco makes a self-serving sales forecast and the photographer asks what to do. Hina gives the definite three-person arrangement and asks for both hands; Ume agrees. Hina promise not to let go is met by a terse reply and a mundane toilet objection, and she laughs. The director later reports the decision and costume change and calls the actual shoot. The shared image is neither an effortless leader command nor the result of everyone adopting one sentimental voice. Hina gives the proposal operational form, while Emoco explicit approval and abrasion remain simultaneous. The ordinary reply to never letting go can temper a totalizing physical promise without negating the expressed attachment.
